<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-9FEC7F3F-E2F1-4B99-AB10-A7D8745ECDB9'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Which set of numbers have a total of $14$ and a range of $3$?

-  $3,5,6$
-  $4,4,6$
-  $4,4,7$
-  $4,5,6$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

ABBR: $\text{Mean} = \dfrac {\text{Total of items}} {\text{Number of items}}$

ABBR: $\text{Range} = \text{Highest number} - \text{Lowest number}$


3, 5, 6

Lets verify,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Sum}\     &= 3 + 5 + 6 \\\\
                &= 14 \\\\
\text{Range}\   &= 6 - 3 \\\\
                &= 3 \\\\
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

3, 6, 5

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>rtq_abbr_md_statistics_formula_mean
rtq_abbr_md_statistics_formula_range
<emptyline>
3, 5, 6
<emptyline>

<emptyline>
$

                &= 14 \\\\
\text{Range}\   &= 6 - 3 \\\\
                &= 3 \\\\
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-B5D7103F-D5FB-441C-B28A-4C02EFC1707D'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Bob counts the number of birds visiting his garden every day for a week. The counts were:

$17, 12, 8, 16, 2, 5, 10$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

%empty%

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

%empty%

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<ul class='subquestion lower-alpha'>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

What was the mean score for the seven days?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

ABBR: $\text{Mean} = \dfrac {\text{Total of items}} {\text{Number of items}}$


$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Mean}\    &= \dfrac {\text{Total birds visiting}} {\text{Total days in week}} \\\\
                &= \dfrac {17 + 12 + 8 + 16 + 2 + 5 + 10} {7} \\\\
                &= \dfrac {70} {7} \\\\
                &= \dfrac {7 \times 10} {7} \\\\
                &= \dfrac {\cancel 7 \times 10} {\cancel 7} \\\\
                &= 10 \\\\
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

10

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>rtq_abbr_md_statistics_formula_mean
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Mean}\    &= \dfrac {\text{Total birds visiting}} {\text{Total days in week}} \\\\
                &= \dfrac {17 + 12 + 8 + 16 + 2 + 5 + 10} {7} \\\\
                &= \dfrac {70} {7} \\\\
                &= \dfrac {7 \times 10} {7} \\\\
                &= \dfrac {\cancel 7 \times 10} {\cancel 7} \\\\
                &= 10 \\\\
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>10
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
<li>

<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

A set of four numbers $5, 7, 12$ and $X$ have a mean of $10$. What is $X$?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

ABBR: $\text{Mean} = \dfrac {\text{Total of items}} {\text{Number of items}}$


$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Mean}\  &= \dfrac {\text{Sum of numbers}} {\text{Number of numbers}} \\\\
10            &= \dfrac {5 + 7 + 12 + X} {4} \\\\
10 \times 4   &= 5 + 7 + 12 + X \\\\
24 + X        &= 40 \\\\
X             &= 40 - 24 \\\\
X             &= 16 \\\\
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

16

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>rtq_abbr_md_statistics_formula_mean
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Mean}\  &= \dfrac {\text{Sum of numbers}} {\text{Number of numbers}} \\\\
10            &= \dfrac {5 + 7 + 12 + X} {4} \\\\
10 \times 4   &= 5 + 7 + 12 + X \\\\
24 + X        &= 40 \\\\
X             &= 40 - 24 \\\\
X             &= 16 \\\\
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>16
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-F1E56932-CB02-461E-B942-E010863A089F'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Mr Leong had five pieces of wood, of the following lengths:

$305 \ \text{cm}, \quad 2.95 \ \text{m}, \quad 225 \ \text{cm}, \quad 3.6 \ \text{m}, \quad 3.15 \ \text{m}$

Work out:

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

%empty%

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

%empty%

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<ul class='subquestion lower-alpha'>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

The mean length.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required.

ABBR: $\text{Mean} = \dfrac {\text{Total of items}} {\text{Number of items}}$


As per the question,

$
\begin {aligned}
\text {Mean length}     &= \dfrac {3.05 + 2.95 + 2.25 + 3.60 + 3.15} {5} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {15} {5} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {3 \times 5} {5} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {3 \times \cancel {5}} {\cancel {5}} \\\\
                        &= 3
\end {aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$3$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>rtq_abbr_md_unit_note
rtq_abbr_md_statistics_formula_mean
<emptyline>
As per the question,
<emptyline>
$
\begin {aligned}
\text {Mean length}     &= \dfrac {3.05 + 2.95 + 2.25 + 3.60 + 3.15} {5} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {15} {5} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {3 \times 5} {5} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {3 \times \cancel {5}} {\cancel {5}} \\\\
                        &= 3
\end {aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$3$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

The median length.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin {aligned}
\text {Lengths} &= 3.05, 2.95, 2.25, 3.60, 3.15 \\\\
\text {Lengths} &= 2,25, 2.95, \maroonC
{3.05}, 3.15, 3.60 \\\\
\text {Median}  &= 3.05 \\\\
\end {aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$3.05 \ \text {m}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin {aligned}
\text {Lengths} &= 3.05, 2.95, 2.25, 3.60, 3.15 \\\\
\text {Lengths} &= 2,25, 2.95, \rtq_katex_color_info{3.05}, 3.15, 3.60 \\\\
\text {Median}  &= 3.05 \\\\
\end {aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</li>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

The range of the lengths.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

ABBR: $\text{Range} = \text{Highest number} - \text{Lowest number}$


As per the question,

$
\begin {aligned}
\text {Range}   &= \text {Largest length} - \text {Smallest length} \\\\
                &= 3.60 - 2.25 \\\\
                &= 1.35
\end {aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>

$1.35$

</div>

| Data | Frequency | Cumulative Frequency |

| $10$ |    $1$    |         $1$          |

| $18$ |    $2$    |         $29$         |
| $20$ |    $1$    |         $30$         |
|    | Total $= 30$|                      |

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Let total}\           &= n \\\\
\text{Median's position}\   &= \dfrac {n + 1} {2} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {30 + 1} {2} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {31} {2} \\\\
                            &= 15.5 \\\
\text{Median}\              &= \dfrac {15\text{th number} + 16\text{th number}} {2} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {13 + 14} {2} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {27} {2} \\\\
                            &= 13.5
\end{aligned}
$

<div class='question question'>

A ticket inspector counted how many passengers there were in each carriage of a train. The results were:

$5, 24, 37, 45, 36, 39, 22$ and $16$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

%empty%

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

%empty%

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<ul class='subquestion lower-alpha'>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

Work out the mean (average) number of passangers.

Show how you worked this out.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

ABBR: $\text{Mean} = \dfrac {\text{Total of items}} {\text{Number of items}}$


$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Mean}\  &= \dfrac {\text{Total passengers}} {\text{Number of carriages}} \\\\
              &= \dfrac {5 + 24 + 37 + 45 + 36 + 39 + 22 + 16} {8} \\\\
              &= \dfrac {224} {8} \\\\
              &= \dfrac {8 \times 28} {8} \\\\
              &= \dfrac {\cancel 8 \times 28} {\cancel 8} \\\\
              &= 28
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

28

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>rtq_abbr_md_statistics_formula_mean
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Mean}\  &= \dfrac {\text{Total passengers}} {\text{Number of carriages}} \\\\
              &= \dfrac {5 + 24 + 37 + 45 + 36 + 39 + 22 + 16} {8} \\\\
              &= \dfrac {224} {8} \\\\
              &= \dfrac {8 \times 28} {8} \\\\
              &= \dfrac {\cancel 8 \times 28} {\cancel 8} \\\\
              &= 28
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>28
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

Work out the range of the number of passangers.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

ABBR: $\text{Range} = \text{Highest number} - \text{Lowest number}$


$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Range}\   &= 45 - 5 \\\\
                &= 40
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

40

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>rtq_abbr_md_statistics_formula_range
<emptyline>
$

<div class='answercodeblock'>

</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

She worked out that the median number of passangers was $30$. Show what working she used to get this answer.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Numbers}\            &= 5, 24, 37, 45, 36, 39, 22, 16 \\\\
\text{Ordered numbers}\    &= 5, 16, 22, \maroonC
{24, 36}, 37, 39, 45 \\\\
\text{Median}\             &= \dfrac {24 + 36} {2} \\\\
                           &= \dfrac {60} {2} \\\\
                           &= \dfrac {2 \times 30} {2} \\\\
                           &= \dfrac {\cancel 2 \times 30} {\cancel 2} \\\\
                           &= 30 \\\\
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

25

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Numbers}\            &= 5, 24, 37, 45, 36, 39, 22, 16 \\\\
\text{Ordered numbers}\    &= 5, 16, 22, \rtq_katex_color_info{24, 36}, 37, 39, 45 \\\\
\text{Median}\             &= \dfrac {24 + 36} {2} \\\\
                           &= \dfrac {60} {2} \\\\
                           &= \dfrac {2 \times 30} {2} \\\\
                           &= \dfrac {\cancel 2 \times 30} {\cancel 2} \\\\
                           &= 30 \\\\
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>

<div class='question question'>

Three pupils had an average weight of $35 \ \text{kg}$. 

</div>
<div class='workings'>

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>

<ul class='subquestion lower-alpha'>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

What is the total weight of these three pupils?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

ABBR: $\text{Mean} = \dfrac {\text{Total of items}} {\text{Number of items}}$


$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Mean}\            &= \dfrac {\text{Total weight}} {\text{Number of pupils}} \\\\
\text{Total weight}\    &= \text{Mean}\ \times \text{Number of pupils}\ \\\\
                        &= 35 \text{kg}\ \times 3 \\\\
                        &= 105 \text{kg}\
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

105

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>rtq_abbr_md_statistics_formula_mean
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Mean}\            &= \dfrac {\text{Total weight}} {\text{Number of pupils}} \\\\
\text{Total weight}\    &= \text{Mean}\ \times \text{Number of pupils}\ \\\\
                        &= 35 \text{kg}\ \times 3 \\\\
                        &= 105 \text{kg}\
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>105
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

A fourth pupil, weighing $55 \ \text{kg}$, joins them.

What is the average weight of this group of four pupils?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

ABBR: $\text{Mean} = \dfrac {\text{Total of items}} {\text{Number of items}}$


$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Mean}\            &= \dfrac {\text{Total weight}} {\text{Number of pupils}} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {105 + 55} {4} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {160} {4} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {4 \times 40} {4} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {\cancel 4 \times 40} {\cancel 4} \\\\
                        &= 40
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

40

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>rtq_abbr_md_statistics_formula_mean
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Mean}\            &= \dfrac {\text{Total weight}} {\text{Number of pupils}} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {105 + 55} {4} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {160} {4} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {4 \times 40} {4} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {\cancel 4 \times 40} {\cancel 4} \\\\
                        &= 40
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>40
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
</ul>

<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>

| Number    | Count     |
|:------:   |:-----:    |
|   $1$     |    $1$    |

|   $4$     |    $2$    |

<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

Find the mean of the ages of the dogs. Explain how you did it.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

ABBR: $\text{Mean} = \dfrac {\text{Total of items}} {\text{Number of items}}$


$
\begin {aligned}
\text {Mean}    &= \dfrac {1 + 3 + 4 + 4 + 5 + 6 + 6 + 6 + 10} {9} \\\\
                &= \dfrac {45} {9} \\\\
                &= \dfrac {5 \times 9} {9} \\\\
                &= \dfrac {5 \times \cancel {9}} {\cancel {9}} \\\\
                &= 5
\end {aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$5$

                &= \dfrac {45} {9} \\\\
                &= \dfrac {5 \times 9} {9} \\\\
                &= \dfrac {5 \times \cancel {9}} {\cancel {9}} \\\\
                &= 5
\end {aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-BD836DC2-D748-406B-8174-5E0A9FED1EA0'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Andrew went on a cycling holiday.

The table shows how far he cycled each day. 

|       Monday      |      Tuesday      |     Wednesday     |      Thursday     |
|:-----------------:|:-----------------:|:-----------------:|:-----------------:|
| $32.3\ \text{km}$ | $38.7\ \text{km}$ | $43.5\ \text{km}$ | $45.1\ \text{km}$ |

He claimed; 'On average, I cycled over $\bold{40 \ \text{km}}$ a day'. 

Show that Andrew is wrong.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

ABBR: $\text{Mean} = \dfrac {\text{Total of items}} {\text{Number of items}}$


$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Mean}\ &= \dfrac {\text{Total distance}} {\text{Number of days}} \\\\
&= \dfrac {32.3 + 38.7 + 43.5 + 45.1} {4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {159.6} {4} \\\\
&= 39.9 \neq \red
{40} \\\\
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

39.9

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>rtq_abbr_md_statistics_formula_mean
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Mean}\ &= \dfrac {\text{Total distance}} {\text{Number of days}} \\\\
&= \dfrac {32.3 + 38.7 + 43.5 + 45.1} {4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {159.6} {4} \\\\
&= 39.9 \neq \rtq_katex_color_wrong_answer{40} \\\\
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>39.9
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-37CACC6E-A15F-4A1E-B3EA-A6DEBA883DAA'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Which two numbers have a **mean** of $\bold{10}$ and a **range** of $\bold{8}$?

The numbers are $\Box$ and $\Box$ 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

ABBR: $\text{Mean} = \dfrac {\text{Total of items}} {\text{Number of items}}$


Let first number $= a$

Let second number $= b$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Mean}\    &= \dfrac {a + b} {\text{2}} \\\\
10              &= \dfrac {a + b} {\text{2}} \\\\
a + b           &= 10 \times 2 \\\\
a + b           &= 20 \\\\
b               &= 20 - a \\\\
\text{Range}    &= a - b \\\\
a - b           &= 8 \\\\
a - (20 - a)    &= 8 \\\\
a - 20 + a      &= 8 \\\\
2 \times a - 20 &= 8 \\\\
2a - 20         &= 8 \\\\
2a              &= 8 + 20 \\\\
2a              &= 28 \\\\
a               &= \dfrac {28} {2} \\\\
a               &= \dfrac {2 \times 14} {2} \\\\
a               &= \dfrac {\cancel 2 \times 14} {\cancel 2} \\\\
a               &= 14 \\\\
b               &= 20 - a \\\\
b               &= 20 - 14 \\\\
b               &= 6 \\\\
\end{aligned}
$

Numbers $= 6, 14$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

6, 14

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>rtq_abbr_md_statistics_formula_mean
<emptyline>
Let first number $= a$
<emptyline>
Let second number $= b$
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Mean}\    &= \dfrac {a + b} {\text{2}} \\\\
10              &= \dfrac {a + b} {\text{2}} \\\\
a + b           &= 10 \times 2 \\\\
a + b           &= 20 \\\\
b               &= 20 - a \\\\
\text{Range}    &= a - b \\\\
a - b           &= 8 \\\\
a - (20 - a)    &= 8 \\\\
a - 20 + a      &= 8 \\\\
2 \times a - 20 &= 8 \\\\
2a - 20         &= 8 \\\\
2a              &= 8 + 20 \\\\
2a              &= 28 \\\\
a               &= \dfrac {28} {2} \\\\
a               &= \dfrac {2 \times 14} {2} \\\\
a               &= \dfrac {\cancel 2 \times 14} {\cancel 2} \\\\
a               &= 14 \\\\
b               &= 20 - a \\\\
b               &= 20 - 14 \\\\
b               &= 6 \\\\
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
Numbers $= 6, 14$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>6, 14
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class='question question'>

Four boys played in a cricket match. Their batting scores were $26, 13, 4$ and $19$.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

%empty%

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

%empty%

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<ul class='subquestion lower-alpha'>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

What was the average (mean) score?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

ABBR: $\text{Mean} = \dfrac {\text{Total of items}} {\text{Number of items}}$


$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Mean} &= \dfrac {\text{Sum of scores}} {\text{Total number of scores}} \\\\
            &= \dfrac {26 + 13 + 4 + 19} {4} \\\\
            &= \dfrac {62} {4} \\\\
            &= 15.5
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

15.5

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>rtq_abbr_md_statistics_formula_mean
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Mean} &= \dfrac {\text{Sum of scores}} {\text{Total number of scores}} \\\\
            &= \dfrac {26 + 13 + 4 + 19} {4} \\\\
            &= \dfrac {62} {4} \\\\
            &= 15.5
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>15.5
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

Another boy, Sam, batted next. The average (mean) score for the five boys is now $16$. What was Sam’s score?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

ABBR: $\text{Mean} = \dfrac {\text{Total of items}} {\text{Number of items}}$


Let Sam's score $= a$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Mean} &= \dfrac {\text{Sum of scores}} {\text{Total number of scores}} \\\\
16          &= \dfrac {26 + 13 + 4 + 19 + a} {5} \\\\
16          &= \dfrac {62 + a} {5} \\\\
62 + a      &= 16 \times 5 \\\\
62 + a      &= 80 \\\\
a           &= 80 - 62 \\\\
a           &= 18 \\\\
\end{aligned}
$

Score of Sam $= 18$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

18

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>rtq_abbr_md_statistics_formula_mean
<emptyline>
Let Sam's score $= a$
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Mean} &= \dfrac {\text{Sum of scores}} {\text{Total number of scores}} \\\\
16          &= \dfrac {26 + 13 + 4 + 19 + a} {5} \\\\
16          &= \dfrac {62 + a} {5} \\\\
62 + a      &= 16 \times 5 \\\\
62 + a      &= 80 \\\\
a           &= 80 - 62 \\\\
a           &= 18 \\\\
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
Score of Sam $= 18$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>18
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
</ul>

<ul class='subquestion lower-alpha'>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

Petra asks five friends how much time they spent watching TV the day before. The answers were $2, 3, 4, 5$ and $8 \ \text{hours}$.

What is the average amount of time watching TV?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

ABBR: $\text{Mean} = \dfrac {\text{Total of items}} {\text{Number of items}}$


$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Mean}\    &= \dfrac {2 + 3 + 4 + 5 + 8} {5} \\\\
                &= \dfrac {22} {5} \\\\
                &= \dfrac {22 \times 2} {5 \times 2} \\\\
                &= \dfrac {44} {10} \\\\
                &= 4.4 \\\\
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>rtq_abbr_md_statistics_formula_mean
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Mean}\    &= \dfrac {2 + 3 + 4 + 5 + 8} {5} \\\\
                &= \dfrac {22} {5} \\\\
                &= \dfrac {22 \times 2} {5 \times 2} \\\\
                &= \dfrac {44} {10} \\\\
                &= 4.4 \\\\
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>


</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

Valeri asks twenty friends the same question as Petra.

She records their answers in a table.

![missing table](/papers/missing_table.svg)


What is the total amount of TV watched by Valeri's $20$ friends?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Total}\   &= (1 \times 5) + (2 \times 5) + (3 \times 6) + (4 \times 4) \\\\
                &= 5 + 10 + 18 + 16 \\\\
                &= 49
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

49

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

                &= 5 + 10 + 18 + 16 \\\\
                &= 49

<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

What is the average time Valeri's friends watched TV?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

ABBR: $\text{Mean} = \dfrac {\text{Total of items}} {\text{Number of items}}$


$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Mean}\    &= \dfrac {\text{Total TV watched}} {\text{Number of friends}} \\\\
                &= \dfrac {49} {20} \\\\
                &= \dfrac {49 \times 5} {20 \times 5} \\\\
                &= \dfrac {196} {100} \\\\
                &= 1.96 \\\\
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

1.96

</div>
</div>

\end{matrix}
$

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-6CCBAA56-EE96-4328-95FF-B7D6CB2D9919'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

The mean shoe size for six boys was $5$. Five of the boys had sizes $3, 7, 6, 2, 3$. What was the shoe size of the sixth boy?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

ABBR: $\text{Mean} = \dfrac {\text{Total of items}} {\text{Number of items}}$


Let shoe size of the sixth boy $= a$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Mean}\    &= \dfrac {3 + 7 + 6 + 2 + 3 + a} {6} \\\\
5               &= \dfrac {21 + a} {6} \\\\
21 + a          &= 5 \times 6 \\\\
21 + a          &= 30 \\\\
a               &= 30 - 21 \\\\
a               &= 7 \\\\
\end{aligned}
$

Shoe size of the sixth boy $= 7$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$7$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

a               &= 7 \\\\
\end{aligned}
$

</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

$

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-E2A7C6BC-C697-4F2E-B9AE-D05C43EEFF4A'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

The mean shoe size for six boys was $5$. Five of the boys had sizes $4, 5, 6, 6, 3$. What was the shoe size of the sixth boy?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

ABBR: $\text{Mean} = \dfrac {\text{Total of items}} {\text{Number of items}}$


Let shoe size of sixth boy $= a$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Mean}\    &= \dfrac {4 + 5 + 6 + 6 + 3 + a} {6} \\\\
5               &= \dfrac {24 + a} {6} \\\\
24 + a          &= 5 \times 6 \\\\
24 + a          &= 30 \\\\
a               &= 30 - 24 \\\\
a               &= 6
\end{aligned}
$

Shoe size of sixth boy $= 6$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$6$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>rtq_abbr_md_statistics_formula_mean
<emptyline>
Let shoe size of sixth boy $= a$
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Mean}\    &= \dfrac {4 + 5 + 6 + 6 + 3 + a} {6} \\\\
5               &= \dfrac {24 + a} {6} \\\\
24 + a          &= 5 \times 6 \\\\
24 + a          &= 30 \\\\
a               &= 30 - 24 \\\\
a               &= 6
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
Shoe size of sixth boy $= 6$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-BEFF5780-107B-47A4-818A-4894859F6406'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

![missing image](/papers/missing_image.svg)


Which day has the median temperature?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Temperatures}\ &= -6, -5, 4, -2, -1 \\\\
\text{Ordered temperatures}\ &= -6. -5, \maroonC
{-2}, -1, 4 \\\\
\text{Median temperature} &= -2 \\\\
\end{aligned}
$

Therefore, Thursday has median temperature.

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

Thursday

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Temperatures}\ &= -6, -5, 4, -2, -1 \\\\
\text{Ordered temperatures}\ &= -6. -5, \rtq_katex_color_info{-2}, -1, 4 \\\\
\text{Median temperature} &= -2 \\\\
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
Therefore, Thursday has median temperature.
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>Thursday
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-730AE050-56B3-4767-99F8-E08D92BFFFD3'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

The mean of $6$ numbers is $9$. Five of the numbers are $3, 6, 7, 8, 6$. What is the $6 \text{th}$ number?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

ABBR: $\text{Mean} = \dfrac {\text{Total of items}} {\text{Number of items}}$


Let sixth number $= a$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Mean}\ &= \dfrac {3 + 6 + 7 + 8 + 6 + a} {6} \\\\
9 &= \dfrac {30 + a} {6} \\\\
30 + a &= 6 \times 9 \\\\
30 + a &= 54 \\\\
a &= 54 - 30 \\\\
a &= 24 \\\\
\end{aligned}
$

Sixth number $= 24$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$24$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>rtq_abbr_md_statistics_formula_mean
<emptyline>
Let sixth number $= a$
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Mean}\ &= \dfrac {3 + 6 + 7 + 8 + 6 + a} {6} \\\\
9 &= \dfrac {30 + a} {6} \\\\
30 + a &= 6 \times 9 \\\\
30 + a &= 54 \\\\
a &= 54 - 30 \\\\
a &= 24 \\\\
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
Sixth number $= 24$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$24$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class='question question'>

The marks in a Maths test were:

$
\begin{matrix}
25 & 26 & 26 & 22 & 28 & 23 \\
27 & 25 & 26 & 27 & 25 & 26 \\
28 & 29 & 24 & 25 & 26 & 29 \\
25 & 26 & 27 & 23 & 23 & 21 \\
\end{matrix}
$

What is the mode of these numbers?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

| Data | Frequency |
| :--: | :-------: |
| $21$ |    $1$    |
| $22$ |    $1$    |
| $23$ |    $3$    |
| $24$ |    $1$    |
| $25$ |    $5$    |
| $26$ |    $6$    |
| $27$ |    $3$    |
| $28$ |    $2$    |
| $29$ |    $2$    |

Mode $= 26$


</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

26

</div>
</div>

<emptyline>

<emptyline>
</code></pre>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-F6421DDF-D209-4B1F-A494-4BF8366EFFE0'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

The mean shoe size for six boys was $5$. Five of the boys had sizes $4, 5, 6, 7, 4$. What was the shoe size of the sixth boy?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

ABBR: $\text{Mean} = \dfrac {\text{Total of items}} {\text{Number of items}}$


Let the size of sixth boy $= a$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Mean}\    &= \dfrac {4 + 5 + 6 + 7 + 4 + a} {6} \\\\
5               &= \dfrac {26 + a} {6} \\\\
26 + a          &= 5 \times 6 \\\\
26 + a          &= 30 \\\\
a               &= 30 - 26 \\\\
a               &= 4
\end{aligned}
$

The size of sixth boy $= 4$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

4

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>rtq_abbr_md_statistics_formula_mean
<emptyline>
Let the size of sixth boy $= a$
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Mean}\    &= \dfrac {4 + 5 + 6 + 7 + 4 + a} {6} \\\\
5               &= \dfrac {26 + a} {6} \\\\
26 + a          &= 5 \times 6 \\\\
26 + a          &= 30 \\\\
a               &= 30 - 26 \\\\
a               &= 4
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
The size of sixth boy $= 4$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>4
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-60EB1E1C-83CC-49EC-8A4E-63559B476793'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

![missing table](/papers/missing_table.svg)


The table shows midday temperatures. What is the average midday temperature?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

ABBR: $\text{Mean} = \dfrac {\text{Total of items}} {\text{Number of items}}$


$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Mean}\ &= \dfrac {\text{Sum of temperatures}} {\text{Number of days}} \\\\
&= \dfrac {15 + 13 + 12 + 9 + 16} {5} \\\\
&= \dfrac {65} {5} \\\\
&= \dfrac {5 \times 13} {5} \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel 5 \times 13} {\cancel 5} \\\\
&= 13
\end{aligned}
$

Mean midday temperature $= 13$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$13$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>rtq_abbr_md_statistics_formula_mean
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Mean}\ &= \dfrac {\text{Sum of temperatures}} {\text{Number of days}} \\\\
&= \dfrac {15 + 13 + 12 + 9 + 16} {5} \\\\
&= \dfrac {65} {5} \\\\
&= \dfrac {5 \times 13} {5} \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel 5 \times 13} {\cancel 5} \\\\
&= 13
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
Mean midday temperature $= 13$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$13$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-4DB301EE-FBA7-4B16-9555-D99D9D2A51DC'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Peter Pilgrim has scored a goal in each of his last $4$ football games. On average he has taken $23$ minutes to score.

He scores again in the next game after $33 \ \text{minutes}$. 

What will the average time to score be for all five games?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

ABBR: $\text{Mean} = \dfrac {\text{Total of items}} {\text{Number of items}}$


$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Mean}\    &= \dfrac {\text{Total time}} {\text{Total days}} \\\\
                &= \dfrac {4 \times 23 + 33} {5} \\\\
                &= \dfrac {92 + 33} {5} \\\\
                &= \dfrac {125} {5} \\\\
                &= \dfrac {5 \times 25} {5} \\\\
                &= \dfrac {\cancel 5 \times 25} {\cancel 5} \\\\
                &= 25 \\\\
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$25$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>rtq_abbr_md_statistics_formula_mean
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Mean}\    &= \dfrac {\text{Total time}} {\text{Total days}} \\\\
                &= \dfrac {4 \times 23 + 33} {5} \\\\
                &= \dfrac {92 + 33} {5} \\\\
                &= \dfrac {125} {5} \\\\
                &= \dfrac {5 \times 25} {5} \\\\
                &= \dfrac {\cancel 5 \times 25} {\cancel 5} \\\\
                &= 25 \\\\
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$25$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-ECDD12D4-46EA-434A-BEFB-8B078113878C'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

I buy $6$ bags of chocolate coins from a shop and count the number of coins in each bag. The quantities were as follows: 

$8, \quad 7, \quad 11, \quad 6, \quad 9, \quad 7$ 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

%empty%

</div>
</div>

</div>

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$7$

</div>
</div>

</code></pre>

</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

Work out the mean (average) of the number of chocolate coins.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

ABBR: $\text{Mean} = \dfrac {\text{Total of items}} {\text{Number of items}}$


$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Mean}    &= \dfrac { 6 + 7 + 7 + 8 + 9 + 11} {6} \\\\
                &= \dfrac {48} {6} \\\\
                &= \dfrac {6 \times 8} {6} \\\\
                &= \dfrac {\cancel 6 \times 8} {\cancel 6} \\\\
                &= 8
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$8$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>rtq_abbr_md_statistics_formula_mean
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Mean}    &= \dfrac { 6 + 7 + 7 + 8 + 9 + 11} {6} \\\\
                &= \dfrac {48} {6} \\\\
                &= \dfrac {6 \times 8} {6} \\\\
                &= \dfrac {\cancel 6 \times 8} {\cancel 6} \\\\
                &= 8
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>

<div class='question question'>

In five of his mid-term tests, each out of $10$ marks, Darshan scores $9$ in Mathematics, $6$ in English, $8$ in Geography, 
$4$ in History, and $8$ in Science.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

%empty%

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>

</div>
</div>

</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<ul class='subquestion lower-alpha'>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

Write down the mode of Darshan's five scores.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$8$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$8$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$8$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>

</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>

</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

Work out the range of marks that Darshan scores.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

ABBR: $\text{Range} = \text{Highest number} - \text{Lowest number}$


$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Range}    &= 9 - 4 \\\\
                &= 5
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$5$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>rtq_abbr_md_statistics_formula_range
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Range}    &= 9 - 4 \\\\
                &= 5
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$5$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

Calculate the mean average score that he achieved in these five tests.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

ABBR: $\text{Mean} = \dfrac {\text{Total of items}} {\text{Number of items}}$


$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Mean} &= \dfrac {9 + 6 + 8 + 4 + 8} {5} \\\\
            &= \dfrac {35} {5} \\\\\
            &= 7 
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$7$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>rtq_abbr_md_statistics_formula_mean
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Mean} &= \dfrac {9 + 6 + 8 + 4 + 8} {5} \\\\
            &= \dfrac {35} {5} \\\\\
            &= 7 
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$7$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

After his French mark are announced, Darshan's average score from all six tests falls to $6.5$ marks. 
Calculate the mark he scored in his French test.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

Let the marks in French $=a$,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Mean}         &= 6.5 \\\\
\dfrac {35 + a} {6} &= 6.5 \\\\
35 + a              &= 6.5 \times 6 \\\\
35 + a              &= 39 \\\\
a                   &= 39 - 35 \\\\
a                   &= 4
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$4$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>Let the marks in French $=a$,
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Mean}         &= 6.5 \\\\
\dfrac {35 + a} {6} &= 6.5 \\\\
35 + a              &= 6.5 \times 6 \\\\
35 + a              &= 39 \\\\
a                   &= 39 - 35 \\\\
a                   &= 4
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$4$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

The pass mark for each test is $50\%$. Write down what fraction of Darshan's six tests (including his French) he managed to pass, leaving your answer in its simplest form.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Total Subjects}\  &= 6 \\\\
\text{Passed subjects}\ &= 4 \\\\
\text{Fraction}\        &= \dfrac {\text{Total Subjects}} {\text{Passed subjects}} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {4} {6} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {2 \times 2} {3 \times 2} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {2 \times \cancel {2} } {3 \times \cancel {2} } \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {2} {3}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\dfrac {2} {3}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Total Subjects}\  &= 6 \\\\
\text{Passed subjects}\ &= 4 \\\\
\text{Fraction}\        &= \dfrac {\text{Total Subjects}} {\text{Passed subjects}} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {4} {6} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {2 \times 2} {3 \times 2} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {2 \times \cancel {2} } {3 \times \cancel {2} } \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {2} {3}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$\dfrac {2} {3}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

In the end term tests, Darshan scores $15$ out of $20$ in his History.

Work out what his percentage improvement is in this subject.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Midterm's percentage marks}   &= \dfrac {4} {10} \times 100 \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {4 \times 100} {10} \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {4 \times 10\cancel0} {1\cancel0} \\\\
                                    &= 4 \times 10 \% \\\\
                                    &= 40 \% \\\\
\text{Endterm's percentage marks}   &= \dfrac {15} {20} \times 100 \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {15 \times 100} {20} \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {15 \times 5 \times 20} {20} \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {15 \times 5 \times \cancel {20}} {\cancel {20}} \\\\
                                    &= 15 \times 5 \% \\\\
                                    &= 75 \% \\\\
\text{Percentage improvement}\      &= 75 \% - 40 \% \\\\
                                    &= 35\% \\\\
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$35\%$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Midterm's percentage marks}   &= \dfrac {4} {10} \times 100 \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {4 \times 100} {10} \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {4 \times 10\cancel0} {1\cancel0} \\\\
                                    &= 4 \times 10 \% \\\\
                                    &= 40 \% \\\\
\text{Endterm's percentage marks}   &= \dfrac {15} {20} \times 100 \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {15 \times 100} {20} \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {15 \times 5 \times 20} {20} \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {15 \times 5 \times \cancel {20}} {\cancel {20}} \\\\
                                    &= 15 \times 5 \% \\\\
                                    &= 75 \% \\\\
\text{Percentage improvement}\      &= 75 \% - 40 \% \\\\
                                    &= 35\% \\\\
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$35\%$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
</ul>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-C6F1CC0D-D31A-4B7A-8872-C20E463CC066'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

On the five school days of a given week, Joshua recorded how many minutes late his school bus arrived each morning. Here is what he recorded:

|    Day    |         Time         |
|:---------:|:--------------------:|
|   Monday  |  $5 \text{ minutes}$ |
|  Tuesday  |  $8 \text{ minutes}$ |
| Wednesday |  $4 \text{ minutes}$ |
|  Thursday | $10 \text{ minutes}$ |
|   Friday  |  $8 \text{ minutes}$ |

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

%empty%

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

%empty%

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<ul class='subquestion lower-alpha'>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

Write down the mode of the amount of minutes the bus was late over the week. 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$8 \ \text{minutes}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$8 \ \text{minutes}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$8 \ \text{minutes}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$8 \ \text{minutes}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

Work out the range of the five times that Joshua recorded.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

ABBR: $\text{Range} = \text{Highest number} - \text{Lowest number}$


$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Range} &= 10 - 4 \\\\
              &= 6
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$6$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>rtq_abbr_md_statistics_formula_range
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Range} &= 10 - 4 \\\\
              &= 6
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$6$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

Calculate the mean average time that the bus was late over the course of the week.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

ABBR: $\text{Mean} = \dfrac {\text{Total of items}} {\text{Number of items}}$


$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Mean} &= \dfrac {5 + 8 + 4 + 10 + 8} {5} \\\\
             &= \dfrac {35} {5} \\\\
             &= 7
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$7$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>rtq_abbr_md_statistics_formula_mean
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Mean} &= \dfrac {5 + 8 + 4 + 10 + 8} {5} \\\\
             &= \dfrac {35} {5} \\\\
             &= 7
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$7$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

The following Monday, Joshua again records how many minutes late the bus arrives, combining this with his results 
from the previous week, the mean average late time is now $6 \ \text{minutes}$.

How much was the bus late by on that second Monday?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

Let the delay on second Monday is $=a$

$
\begin{aligned}
\dfrac {35 + a} {6} &= 6 \\\\
35 + a              &= 6 \times 6 \\\\
a                   &= 36 - 35 \\\\
a                   &= 1
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$1$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>Let the delay on second Monday is $=a$
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\dfrac {35 + a} {6} &= 6 \\\\
35 + a              &= 6 \times 6 \\\\
a                   &= 36 - 35 \\\\
a                   &= 1
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$1$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

Joshua keeps recording this information each morning. By the end of Thursday of the second week, the mean average has now fallen to 
$4 \ \text{minutes}$.

Explain what this tells us about the number of minutes that the bus was late over the middle three days of the second week.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

Let the total for middle three days $= a$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Mean}\    &= \dfrac {36 + a} {9} \\\\
4               &= \dfrac {36 + a} {9} \\\\
36 + a          &= 4 \times 9 \\\\
36 + a          &= 36 \\\\
a               &= 36 - 36 \\\\
a               &= 0 \\\\
\end{aligned}
$

The bus never came late in the middle $3$ days of second week.

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

The bus never came late in the middle $3$ days of second week.

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>Let the total for middle three days $= a$
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Mean}\    &= \dfrac {36 + a} {9} \\\\
4               &= \dfrac {36 + a} {9} \\\\
36 + a          &= 4 \times 9 \\\\
36 + a          &= 36 \\\\
a               &= 36 - 36 \\\\
a               &= 0 \\\\
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
The bus never came late in the middle $3$ days of second week.
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>The bus never came late in the middle $3$ days of second week.
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
</ul>
